- **corpus-ingest**: Parses whatever export files are present in exports/ and loads them into the unified local corpus database. Auto-detects file types, routes each to the right parser, dedupes on re-runs, supports delta mode for ongoing scans, and prints a coverage report. The user wants a separate interactive agent session they can attach to, not a built-in subagent or Agent/Task tool, and not your own answer, even if you are Claude Code or Codex yourself.
